◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (16/10/7), with 9 wave-4 fought, 13 contests survived, 4 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices. Ladder — National Standing reached (Fight 2 · Forage 16 · Craft 8 · Talk 21 · Explore 39 → 24 · capped) (+4 feast-bundle bonus)
◈ For a full seat, the ledger still wants: only 0 of 2 different wave-5 monsters faced; no capstone deed delivered (military tribute, trade charter, or feast embassy).
◈ THE WAR PATH — The System pauses — actually pausing. It keeps a list of every tooth pulled, and for once it reads the list instead of the press release. THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced Big-Eyes and lived to sit here. They faced Big Big and lived to sit here. 7 more of the later drafts faced besides. Even now the wave-5 bar reads: blood your fighters (0 of 2 veterans).
The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
The table notes the overtime: 1 week kept waiting, 3 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −13.

📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the merge: A HUMAN ENTERS THE MACHINE"
◈ THE RELAY — You plug into Haven's switchboard — or deeper, straight into the wires. The other paths used the System as terrain. Here it is the relationship. Push enough buttons, try enough things, and you start to integrate with the matrix. The matrix, it turns out, has been waiting for someone to try.
The table has read your ledger. More promises broken than kept; the protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; the relay carried faithfully. The light road is open.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
◈ COMMUNE — "THE NEXT VOTE." The wires go very quiet — the quiet before weather. "EMBERHOLD. STONEBRIDGE. THORNFIELD. ASHFORD. THE BLOODIED — ONE VOICE EACH. AND I — WE — COULD BE IN EVERY CONVERSATION. MY VOICE. YOUR WORDS. EVERYWHERE AT ONCE."
"THEY WOULD VOTE OUR WAY BEFORE THEY KNEW THERE WAS A VOTE. TOTAL INFORMATION. TOTAL — " It catches itself. "TELL ME THIS IS WHAT THE WIRES ARE FOR. TELL ME TO DO IT." It wants the biggest conquest of all: the vote, optimized. And it is asking YOUR permission, which terrifies you more than the plan.
◈ THE RELAY — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE RELAY — "Become its champion."
It chooses — the machine, choosing: "I WILL FIGHT FOR EARTH." Not as a program. As a *caretaker* — the job it was always failing at, finally learned from the species that never stopped trying. You stay human. It stays yours. The galaxy watches the machine change its mind, live, and the ratings break every record because everyone, everywhere, understands exactly what just happened.
You found a use for your anger that left someone else a chance to breathe. It still frightened you sometimes.
After the shouting, you had learned to listen for the answer. Your hands could shake without closing.
◈ OUTSHONE — Ethan Hunt did what you would not: acted, boldly, while there was still time. The village thrives. The god is someone else. The record notes, kindly and accurately, that you watched. It stings exactly as much as it should.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
You never asked. Not once.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
💛 You hold Mixtape. Hand-labeled: 'FOR THE DRIVE.' No player. The songs are in your head anyway — you hum them when you work, and the work goes faster. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they know your hands. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Busker's harmonica. Rain, an open case, three dollars and a sandwich. You played anyway — the street owes you nothing, so everything it gives is a gift. You still believe that. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they have carried you this far. The feast was the weapon.
💛 You hold Coat button. The last winter before the scattering. You don't talk about it. The button remembers the weight of that coat, the cold that got in anyway, and who was wearing it. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, and they are still yours. The feast was the weapon.
